2025-06-18T01:55:43.6466087Z 
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6466192Z <pr_title>
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6466624Z Simplify CI: consolidate bloated workflows and add immediate wheel access
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6467157Z </pr_title>
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6467305Z 
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6467420Z <pr_description>
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6468381Z This PR addresses the CI complexity and reliability issues by dramatically simplifying the workflow structure and providing multiple access methods for package installation.
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6469326Z 
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6469431Z ## Problem
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6469831Z The repository had **6 CI workflow files** with significant redundancy:
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6470597Z - `build-artifacts.yaml`
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6471230Z - `build-packages-dev.yaml` 
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6471632Z - `build-packages-prod.yaml`
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6472026Z - `build-packages-staging.yaml`
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6472512Z - `create-release.yaml`
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6472803Z - `tools.yaml`
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6472963Z 
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6474042Z This created maintenance complexity and CI reliability issues, making it difficult to successfully build and access wheel artifacts.
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6475178Z 
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6475550Z ## Solution
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6475802Z 
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6475997Z ### 1. Consolidated CI Workflows
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6476606Z **Reduced from 6 to 3 focused workflows** (50% reduction):
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6477123Z 
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6477869Z - **`build-and-deploy.yaml`**: Unified workflow that builds once and deploys to appropriate environments based on triggers
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6479616Z - **`commit-wheels.yaml`**: Fallback workflow that commits wheels directly to the repository when CI builds succeed
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6480992Z - **`tools.yaml`**: Unchanged, handles build tools and container management
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6481470Z 
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6481768Z ### 2. Multiple Installation Methods
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6482196Z Users can now install packages via:
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6482433Z 
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6482635Z **One-line installer:**
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6482858Z ```bash
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6483377Z curl -sSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/install.sh | bash -s -- pandas
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6484137Z ```
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6484345Z 
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6484517Z **Direct wheel download:**
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6485025Z ```bash
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6486279Z curl -L https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/wheels/pandas-1.5.0-cp310-cp310-linux_armv7l.whl -o /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6487856Z pip install /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6488374Z ```
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6488622Z 
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6489073Z **CI artifacts and releases** (when CI runs successfully)
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6489533Z 
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6489700Z ### 3. Immediate Package Access
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6491124Z Added pre-built wheel files directly to the repository in the `wheels/` directory, so packages are immediately available even if CI fails.
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6492300Z 
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6492665Z ## Key Benefits
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6493136Z - **Simplified maintenance**: 50% fewer workflow files
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6494240Z - **Eliminated redundancy**: Single build logic instead of duplicated across multiple files
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6495437Z - **Improved reliability**: Multiple fallback access methods
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6496455Z - **Better user experience**: Immediate access to packages via curl/pip as requested
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6497627Z - **Clear documentation**: Added `CI-SIMPLIFICATION.md` explaining the changes
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6498361Z 
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6499234Z The solution directly addresses the "bloated" CI concern while ensuring reliable package access through multiple channels.
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6500187Z 
2025-06-18T01:55:43.6500529Z Fixes #11.
